AA PART IV. NOTIFICATIONS, During the Quarter ended December S1, 1908. 04
CHINA SEA. No. 386. YANGTZE RIVER--KIUKIANG DISTRICT. CHUANKIANGKOW BEACON-LIGHT ESTABLISHED. NoTicE is hereby given that the Chuankiangkow Beacon-light was exhibited for the first time on the Ixi th October 1903. The illuminating apparatus is dioptric, of the sixth order, showing a fixed red light. The beacon is placed on the point on the left bank of the river, immediately opposite the 1 end of Jocelyn Island. SHANGHAI, 2oth October 1903. No. 387. SHANGHAI DISTRIOT. YANGTZE RIVER: HARVEY POINT AND CONFUCIUS CHANNELS, AND THE CENTAUR AND LANGSHAN CROSSINGS. ALTERATIONS IN BUOYAGE. NOTICE is hereby given that the Mason Bank Buoy has been shifted to a position from which the Mason Point Beacon bears N. S8&#39; io&#39; , and is distant 2.9 miles. That the Actaeon Shoal Upper Buoy has been altered to red and now marks the northern side of the channel.. That the Confucius Channel and Centaur Crossing having again opened, they have been marked by the following buoys:- The S.E Spit gas-lighted buoy has been altered to a spit buoy, i.e., has been painted in red and black vertical stripes and marks the south-eastern entrances to both the Harvey-Point and Confucius Channels. r *
HTIhl:Dorgs-Nest Buoy, &amp;amp;,6-foot-oDniMal red buoy surmounted by a spherical cage, has been moored in 35 feet of water and marks: the seuthern edge of the Dove&#39;s Nest Shoal. From the buoy the Leo Point Beacon bears S. 1 151W., and is distant 1.45 miles. The Acton Buoy, a 6-foot conical ed buoy surmounted by a cylindrical shape, has been moored in 33 feet of water and marks the soutl-western edge of the Actawon Shoal. From the buoy the Fork Tree Bacon bears N. 66&#39; W., and is distant 5.4 miles. The Centaur Lower Buoy, a 6-foot conical black buoy surmounted by a triangular shape, has been moored in 37 feet of water and marks the south-eastern edge of the Centaur Shoal. From the buoy the Fork Tree Beacon bears N. 8o* 15&#39; W., and is distant 3.45 miles. The Centaur Upper Buoy, a 6-foot conical black buoy surmounted by a spherical cage, has been moored in 35 feet of water aiid marks the upper qud of the Centaur Crossing. From the buoy the Fork Tree Beacon bears S. 72&#39; W., and is distant 2.3 miles. And that the Vine Point Lower Buoy has been shifted to a position from which the Fushan Pagoda bears S. 69&#39; 40&#39; W., and is distant 2.6 miles. All depths given are for low water of spring tides, and all bearings are magnetic. SHANGHAI, I8th Nfovembe, 1903. No. 388. SHANGHAI AND NINGPO DISTRICTS. WEST VOLCANO AND SQUARE ISLAND LIGHTS. ALTERATINoS IN CHARACTER OF LIGHTS. NOTICE is hereby given that the followiqg alterations have been made in the ebaracter of the West Volcano and Square Island Lights:- West Yolcano has been altered from a fixed to a group-occulting waite light, making two eclipses in quick succession every 20 seconds, thus: Light . . . . . . . . . . . 13 seconds.
